00018: function clampProb(v) {
00019:   const n = Number(v);
00020:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
00021:   if (n > 1) return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / 100));
00022:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
00023: }
00024: 
00025: function resolveProbValue(x) {
00026:   return clampProb(x?.scoreProb ?? x?.prob ?? x?.probCond ?? 0);
00027: }
00028: 
00029: function normalizeMilhar4(v) {
00030:   const dig = String(v || "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
00031:   if (!dig) return "";
00032:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
00033: }
00034: 
00035: function build4ColsFromEngineOut(out, expectedCols = 4, perCol = 5) {
00036:   const dezenas = Array.isArray(out?.dezenas) ? out.dezenas : [];
00037:   const slots = Array.isArray(out?.slots) ? out.slots : [];
00038:   const cols = [];
00039: 
00040:   for (const dz of dezenas.slice(0, expectedCols)) {
00041:     const items = slots
00042:       .filter((s) => String(s?.dezena || "") === String(dz))
00043:       .map((s) => normalizeMilhar4(s?.milhar))
00044:       .map((m) => (/^\d{4}$/.test(m) ? m : ""))
00045:       .slice(0, perCol);
00046: 
00047:     while (items.length < perCol) items.push("");
00048:     cols.push({ dezena: dz, items });
00049:   }
00050: 
00051:   while (cols.length < expectedCols) {
00052:     cols.push({ dezena: "", items: Array(perCol).fill("") });
00053:   }
00054: 
00055:   return cols.slice(0, expectedCols);
00056: }

00058: function normalizeImgSrc(src, publicBase = "") {
00059:   const s = String(src || "").trim();
00060:   if (!s) return "";
00061:   if (/^(https?:)?\/\//i.test(s)) return s;
00062:   if (/^(data:|blob:)/i.test(s)) return s;
00063: 
00064:   const base = String(publicBase || "").trim().replace(/\/+$/, "");
00065: 
00066:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return `${base}${s}`;
00067:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return `${base}/${s.slice("public/".length)}`;
00068:   return `${base}/${s}`;
00069: }

00109: function top3ItemMatchesSlot(item, slot) {
00110:   const targetY = safeStr(slot?.targetYmd || "");
00111:   const targetH = toHourBucket(slot?.targetHour || "");
00112: 
00113:   const nextY = safeStr(item?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
00114:   const nextH = toHourBucket(item?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
00115: 
00116:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return false;
00117:   if (!isYMD(nextY) || !nextH) return false;
00118: 
00119:   return nextY === targetY && nextH === targetH;
00120: }

03834: /* =========================
03835:    TIMELINE DO DIA (CADEIA PREDITIVA)
03836: ========================= */
03837: 
03838: 
03839: 
03840: export function auditTop3Timeline({
03841:   timeline,
03842:   lotteryKey = "",
03843: }) {
03844:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
03845: 
03846:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
03847:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
03848:     return status === "validated" && Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo));
03849:   });
03850: 
03851:   const total = validated.length;
03852: 
03853:   const top1Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03854:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03855:     const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03856:     return Number.isFinite(first) && first === result;
03857:   }).length;
03858: 
03859:   const top3Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03860:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03861:     return (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03862:       .slice(0, 3)
03863:       .some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result);
03864:   }).length;
03865: 
03866:   function pct(n, d) {
03867:     if (!d) return 0;
03868:     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
03869:   }
03870: 
03871:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
03872:     const map = new Map();
03873: 
03874:     for (const slot of validated) {
03875:       const key = keyFn(slot);
03876:       const current = map.get(key) || {
03877:         key,
03878:         total: 0,
03879:         top1Hits: 0,
03880:         top3Hits: 0,
03881:       };
03882: 
03883:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03884:       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03885:       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
03886: 
03887:       current.total += 1;
03888:       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
03889:       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
03890: 
03891:       map.set(key, current);
03892:     }
03893: 
03894:     return Array.from(map.values())
03895:       .map((x) => ({
03896:         ...x,
03897:         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
03898:         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
03899:       }))
03900:       .sort((a, b) => {
03901:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
03902:         return b.total - a.total;
03903:       });
03904:   }
03905: 
03906:   return {
03907:     lotteryKey,
03908:     total,
03909:     top1Hits,
03910:     top3Hits,
03911:     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
03912:     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
03913:     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
03914:     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
03915:     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
03916:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
03917:       hour: slot.targetHour,
03918:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
03919:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
03920:       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
03921:       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03922:         .slice(0, 3)
03923:         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
03924:       top1Hit:
03925:         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
03926:       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03927:         .slice(0, 3)
03928:         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
03929:     })),
03930:   };
03931: }
03932: 
03933:

03815:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
03816:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
03817:       pushed += 1;
03818:     }
03819:   }
03820: 
03821:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
03822: 
03823:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };

03840: export function auditTop3Timeline({
03841:   timeline,
03842:   lotteryKey = "",
03843: }) {
03844:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
03845: 
03846:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
03847:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
03848:     return status === "validated" && Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo));
03849:   });
03850: 
03851:   const total = validated.length;
03852: 
03853:   const top1Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03854:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03855:     const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03856:     return Number.isFinite(first) && first === result;
03857:   }).length;
03858: 
03859:   const top3Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03860:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03861:     return (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03862:       .slice(0, 3)
03863:       .some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result);
03864:   }).length;
03865: 
03866:   function pct(n, d) {
03867:     if (!d) return 0;
03868:     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
03869:   }
03870: 
03871:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
03872:     const map = new Map();
03873: 
03874:     for (const slot of validated) {
03875:       const key = keyFn(slot);
03876:       const current = map.get(key) || {
03877:         key,
03878:         total: 0,
03879:         top1Hits: 0,
03880:         top3Hits: 0,
03881:       };
03882: 
03883:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03884:       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03885:       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
03886: 
03887:       current.total += 1;
03888:       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
03889:       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
03890: 
03891:       map.set(key, current);
03892:     }
03893: 
03894:     return Array.from(map.values())
03895:       .map((x) => ({
03896:         ...x,
03897:         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
03898:         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
03899:       }))
03900:       .sort((a, b) => {
03901:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
03902:         return b.total - a.total;
03903:       });
03904:   }
03905: 
03906:   return {
03907:     lotteryKey,
03908:     total,
03909:     top1Hits,
03910:     top3Hits,
03911:     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
03912:     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
03913:     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
03914:     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
03915:     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
03916:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
03917:       hour: slot.targetHour,
03918:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
03919:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
03920:       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
03921:       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03922:         .slice(0, 3)
03923:         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
03924:       top1Hit:
03925:         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
03926:       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03927:         .slice(0, 3)
03928:         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
03929:     })),
03930:   };
03931: }

03840: export function auditTop3Timeline({
03841:   timeline,
03842:   lotteryKey = "",
03843: }) {
03844:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
03845: 
03846:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
03847:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
03848:     return status === "validated" && Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo));
03849:   });
03850: 
03851:   const total = validated.length;
03852: 
03853:   const top1Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03854:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03855:     const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03856:     return Number.isFinite(first) && first === result;
03857:   }).length;
03858: 
03859:   const top3Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03860:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03861:     return (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03862:       .slice(0, 3)
03863:       .some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result);
03864:   }).length;
03865: 
03866:   function pct(n, d) {
03867:     if (!d) return 0;
03868:     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
03869:   }
03870: 
03871:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
03872:     const map = new Map();
03873: 
03874:     for (const slot of validated) {
03875:       const key = keyFn(slot);
03876:       const current = map.get(key) || {
03877:         key,
03878:         total: 0,
03879:         top1Hits: 0,
03880:         top3Hits: 0,
03881:       };
03882: 
03883:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03884:       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03885:       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
03886: 
03887:       current.total += 1;
03888:       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
03889:       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
03890: 
03891:       map.set(key, current);
03892:     }
03893: 
03894:     return Array.from(map.values())
03895:       .map((x) => ({
03896:         ...x,
03897:         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
03898:         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
03899:       }))
03900:       .sort((a, b) => {
03901:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
03902:         return b.total - a.total;
03903:       });
03904:   }
03905: 
03906:   return {
03907:     lotteryKey,
03908:     total,
03909:     top1Hits,
03910:     top3Hits,
03911:     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
03912:     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
03913:     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
03914:     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
03915:     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
03916:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
03917:       hour: slot.targetHour,
03918:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
03919:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
03920:       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
03921:       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03922:         .slice(0, 3)
03923:         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
03924:       top1Hit:
03925:         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
03926:       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03927:         .slice(0, 3)
03928:         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
03929:     })),
03930:   };
03931: }

03883:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);

04163: export function auditTop3Backtest({
04164:   drawsRange,
04165:   lotteryKey,
04166:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04167:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04168:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04169: }) {
04170:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
04171:   const dates = Array.from(
04172:     new Set(
04173:       range
04174:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
04175:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
04176:     )

03842:   lotteryKey = "",
03843: }) {
03844:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
03845: 
03846:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
03847:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
03848:     return status === "validated" && Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo));
03849:   });
03850: 
03851:   const total = validated.length;
03852: 
03853:   const top1Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03854:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03855:     const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03856:     return Number.isFinite(first) && first === result;
03857:   }).length;
03858: 
03859:   const top3Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03860:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03861:     return (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03862:       .slice(0, 3)
03863:       .some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result);
03864:   }).length;
03865: 
03866:   function pct(n, d) {
03867:     if (!d) return 0;
03868:     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
03869:   }
03870: 
03871:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
03872:     const map = new Map();
03873: 
03874:     for (const slot of validated) {
03875:       const key = keyFn(slot);
03876:       const current = map.get(key) || {
03877:         key,
03878:         total: 0,
03879:         top1Hits: 0,
03880:         top3Hits: 0,
03881:       };
03882: 
03883:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03884:       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03885:       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
03886: 
03887:       current.total += 1;
03888:       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
03889:       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
03890: 
03891:       map.set(key, current);
03892:     }
03893: 
03894:     return Array.from(map.values())
03895:       .map((x) => ({
03896:         ...x,
03897:         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
03898:         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
03899:       }))
03900:       .sort((a, b) => {
03901:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
03902:         return b.total - a.total;
03903:       });
03904:   }
03905: 
03906:   return {
03907:     lotteryKey,
03908:     total,
03909:     top1Hits,
03910:     top3Hits,
03911:     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
03912:     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
03913:     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
03914:     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
03915:     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
03916:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
03917:       hour: slot.targetHour,
03918:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
03919:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
03920:       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
03921:       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03922:         .slice(0, 3)
03923:         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
03924:       top1Hit:
03925:         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
03926:       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03927:         .slice(0, 3)
03928:         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
03929:     })),
03930:   };
03931: }

03934: function buildTimelineForDate({
03935:   ymd,
03936:   drawsToday,
03937:   drawsRange,
03938:   lotteryKey,
03939:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
03940:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
03941:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
03942: }) {
03943:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
03944:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
03945:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
03946: 
03947:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
03948: 
03949:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
03950:     lotteryKey,
03951:     ymd: targetYmd,
03952:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
03953:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
03954:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
03955:   });
03956: 
03957:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
03958:     .map(toHourBucket)
03959:     .filter(Boolean);
03960: 
03961:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
03962: 
03963:   const daySorted = day
03964:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
03965:     .sort((a, b) => {
03966:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
03967:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
03968:       return ta - tb;

04107:       lotteryKey,
04108:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
04109:       drawLast: baseDraw,
04110:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
04111:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04112:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04113:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04114:       topN: 3,
04115:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
04116:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
04117:     });
04118: 
04119: 
04120:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
04121:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
04122:     const computedForSlot =
04123:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
04124: 
04125:     const top3 =
04126:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
04127:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
04128:         : [];
04129: 
04130:     const resultGrupo = currentDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(currentDraw) : null;
04131: 
04132:     const hit =
04133:       Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) && top3.length
04134:         ? top3.some((t) => Number(t?.grupo) === Number(resultGrupo))
04135:         : null;
04136: 
04137:     timeline.push({
04138:       targetYmd,
04139:       targetHour: slotHour,
04140:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
04141:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
04142:       top3,
04143:       resultGrupo: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? Number(resultGrupo) : null,
04144:       hit,
04145:       status: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? "validated" : "pending",
04146:     });
04147: 
04148:     if (currentDraw) {
04149:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
04150:     } else {
04151:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
04152:     }
04153:   }
04154: 
04155:   return timeline;
04156: 
04157: }

04102:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));

04121:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");

04123:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
04124:

04126:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)

04141:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
04142:       top3,
04143:       resultGrupo: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? Number(resultGrupo) : null,
04144:       hit,
04145:       status: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? "validated" : "pending",

04149:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);

04151:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
04152:     }
04153:   }
04154:

04159: export function buildTimelineTop3(args) {
04160:   return buildTimelineForDate(args || {});
04161: }
04162: 
04163: export function auditTop3Backtest({
04164:   drawsRange,
04165:   lotteryKey,
04166:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04167:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04168:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04169: }) {
04170:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
04171:   const dates = Array.from(
04172:     new Set(
04173:       range
04174:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
04175:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
04176:     )
04177:   ).sort();
04178: 
04179:   const allTimeline = [];
04180: 
04181:   for (const ymd of dates) {
04182:     const drawsToday = range.filter((d) => pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd);
04183: 
04184:     const dayTimeline = buildTimelineForDate({
04185:       ymd,
04186:       drawsToday,
04187:       drawsRange: range,
04188:       lotteryKey,
04189:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04190:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04191:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04192:     });
04193: 
04194:     allTimeline.push(...(Array.isArray(dayTimeline) ? dayTimeline : []));
04195:   }
04196: 
04197:   return auditTop3Timeline({
04198:     timeline: allTimeline,
04199:     lotteryKey,
04200:   });
04201: }

04185:       ymd,

04065:   });
04066: 
04067:   function pct(n, d) {
04068:     if (!d) return 0;
04069: 
04070:     return Number(
04071:       ((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2)
04072:     );
04073:   }
04074: 
04075:   function normalizeResultTop3(slot) {
04076:     const source = Array.isArray(slot?.resultTop3Groups)
04077:       ? slot.resultTop3Groups
04078:       : [];
04079: 
04080:     const normalized = source
04081:       .slice(0, 3)
04082:       .map((grupo) => {
04083:         const value = Number(grupo);
04084: 
04085:         return Number.isFinite(value) &&
04086:           value >= 1 &&
04087:           value <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04088:           ? value
04089:           : null;
04090:       });
04091: 
04092:     while (normalized.length < 3) {
04093:       normalized.push(null);
04094:     }
04095: 
04096:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(normalized[0]))) {
04097:       const fallback = Number(slot?.resultGrupo);
04098: 
04099:       normalized[0] =
04100:         Number.isFinite(fallback) &&
04101:         fallback >= 1 &&
04102:         fallback <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04103:           ? fallback
04104:           : null;
04105:     }
04106: 
04107:     return normalized;
04108:   }
04109: 
04110:   function evaluateSlot(slot) {
04111:     const picks = (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
04112:       .slice(0, 3)
04113:       .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
04114:       .filter(
04115:         (grupo) =>
04116:           Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
04117:           grupo >= 1 &&
04118:           grupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04119:       );
04120: 
04121:     const resultTop3 = normalizeResultTop3(slot);
04122:     const resultGrupo = Number(resultTop3[0]);
04123: 
04124:     const top1Hit =
04125:       picks.length > 0 &&
04126:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
04127:       Number(picks[0]) === resultGrupo;
04128: 
04129:     const top3Hit =
04130:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
04131:       picks.some(
04132:         (grupo) => Number(grupo) === resultGrupo
04133:       );
04134: 
04135:     const prizePositionHits = resultTop3.map(
04136:       (grupo) =>
04137:         Number.isFinite(Number(grupo)) &&
04138:         picks.some(
04139:           (pick) => Number(pick) === Number(grupo)
04140:         )
04141:     );
04142: 
04143:     const predictionHits = picks.map((grupo) =>
04144:       resultTop3.some(
04145:         (result) =>
04146:           Number.isFinite(Number(result)) &&
04147:           Number(result) === Number(grupo)
04148:       )
04149:     );
04150: 
04151:     const matchedPrizePositions =
04152:       prizePositionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
04153: 
04154:     const matchedPredictions =
04155:       predictionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
04156: 
04157:     return {
04158:       picks,
04159:       resultTop3,
04160:       resultGrupo,
04161: 
04162:       top1Hit,
04163:       top3Hit,
04164: 
04165:       prize1Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[0]),
04166:       prize2Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[1]),
04167:       prize3Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[2]),
04168: 
04169:       top3PrizeHit: matchedPrizePositions > 0,
04170: 
04171:       matchedPrizePositions,
04172:       matchedPredictions,
04173: 
04174:       prizePositionHits,
04175:       predictionHits,
04176:     };
04177:   }
04178: 
04179:   const evaluated = validated.map((slot) => ({
04180:     slot,
04181:     metrics: evaluateSlot(slot),
04182:   }));

04341: function buildTimelineForDate({
04342:   ymd,
04343:   drawsToday,
04344:   drawsRange,
04345:   lotteryKey,
04346:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04347:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04348:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04349: }) {
04350:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
04351:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
04352:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
04353: 
04354:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
04355: 
04356:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
04357:     lotteryKey,
04358:     ymd: targetYmd,
04359:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04360:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04361:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04362:   });
04363: 
04364:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
04365:     .map(toHourBucket)
04366:     .filter(Boolean);
04367: 
04368:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
04369: 
04370:   const daySorted = day
04371:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
04372:     .sort((a, b) => {
04373:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04374:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04375:       return ta - tb;
04376:     });
04377: 
04378:   const rangeSorted = range
04379:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
04380:     .sort((a, b) => {
04381:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04382:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04383:       return ta - tb;
04384:     });
04385: 
04386:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
04387: 
04388:   for (const d of daySorted) {
04389:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04390:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04391:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
04392:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
04393:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
04394:   }

04646: export function auditTop3Backtest({
04647:   drawsRange,
04648:   lotteryKey,
04649:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04650:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04651:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04652: }) {

04656:       range

04668:       ymd,

00176: function extractOfficialPodium(draw) {
00177:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes)
00178:     ? draw.prizes
00179:     : [];
00180: 
00181:   return [1, 2, 3].map((position) => {
00182:     const prize =
00183:       prizes.find(
00184:         (item) => Number(item?.position) === position
00185:       ) ||
00186:       prizes[position - 1] ||
00187:       null;
00188: 
00189:     if (!prize) return null;
00190: 
00191:     const grupo = extractPrizeGrupo(prize);
00192:     const milhar = normalizeMilhar(
00193:       prize?.milhar ??
00194:         prize?.numero ??
00195:         prize?.number ??
00196:         prize?.valor ??
00197:         ""
00198:     );
00199: 
00200:     if (
00201:       !Number.isFinite(Number(grupo)) ||
00202:       Number(grupo) < 1 ||
00203:       Number(grupo) > 25
00204:     ) {
00205:       return null;
00206:     }
00207: 
00208:     return {
00209:       position,
00210:       grupo: Number(grupo),
00211:       milhar,
00212:       animal: safeStr(prize?.animal || ""),
00213:     };
00214:   });
00215: }

00221:   return "";

00217: function podiumMedalFromPosition(position) {
00218:   if (Number(position) === 1) return "gold";
00219:   if (Number(position) === 2) return "silver";
00220:   if (Number(position) === 3) return "bronze";
00221:   return "";
00222: }
00223: 
00224: function analyzeSnapshotHit(snapshot, officialPodium) {
00225:   const top3 = Array.isArray(snapshot)
00226:     ? snapshot.slice(0, 3)
00227:     : [];
00228: 
00229:   const podium = Array.isArray(officialPodium)
00230:     ? officialPodium.filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3)
00231:     : [];
00232: 
00233:   if (!top3.length || !podium.length) {
00234:     return {
00235:       hitType: "miss",
00236:       hitScore: 0,
00237:       hitPosition: -1,
00238:       predictionPosition: -1,
00239:       resultPosition: -1,
00240:       podiumMedal: "",
00241:       matchedValue: "",
00242:       matchedGrupo: null,
00243:       matchedMilhar: "",
00244:       matchedAnimal: "",
00245:     };
00246:   }
00247: 
00248:   for (const officialPrize of podium) {
00249:     const resultGrupo = Number(officialPrize?.grupo);
00250:     const resultMilhar = normalizeMilhar(
00251:       officialPrize?.milhar
00252:     );
00253:     const resultCentena = resultMilhar
00254:       ? resultMilhar.slice(-3)
00255:       : "";
00256: 
00257:     for (
00258:       let predictionIndex = 0;
00259:       predictionIndex < top3.length;
00260:       predictionIndex += 1
00261:     ) {
00262:       const prediction = top3[predictionIndex];
00263:       const predictionGrupo = Number(prediction?.grupo);
00264: 
00265:       const milhares = (
00266:         Array.isArray(prediction?.milhares20)
00267:           ? prediction.milhares20
00268:           : Array.isArray(prediction?.milhares)
00269:             ? prediction.milhares
00270:             : []
00271:       )
00272:         .map(normalizeMilhar)
00273:         .filter(Boolean);
00274: 
00275:       const centenas = milhares.map(
00276:         (value) => value.slice(-3)
00277:       );
00278: 
00279:       let hitType = "miss";
00280:       let hitScore = 0;
00281:       let matchedValue = "";
00282: 
00283:       if (
00284:         resultMilhar &&
00285:         milhares.includes(resultMilhar)
00286:       ) {
00287:         hitType = "hit_exact";
00288:         hitScore = 100;
00289:         matchedValue = resultMilhar;
00290:       } else if (
00291:         resultCentena &&
00292:         centenas.includes(resultCentena)
00293:       ) {
00294:         hitType = "hit_centena";
00295:         hitScore = 66.67;
00296:         matchedValue = resultCentena;
00297:       } else if (
00298:         Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
00299:         predictionGrupo === resultGrupo
00300:       ) {
00301:         hitType = "hit_grupo";
00302:         hitScore = 33.33;
00303:         matchedValue = resultMilhar
00304:           ? resultMilhar.slice(-2)
00305:           : String(resultGrupo).padStart(2, "0");
00306:       }
00307: 
00308:       if (hitType !== "miss") {
00309:         const resultPosition = Number(
00310:           officialPrize?.position
00311:         );
00312: 
00313:         return {
00314:           hitType,
00315:           hitScore,
00316:           hitPosition: predictionIndex + 1,
00317:           predictionPosition: predictionIndex + 1,
00318:           resultPosition,
00319:           podiumMedal:
00320:             podiumMedalFromPosition(resultPosition),
00321:           matchedValue,
00322:           matchedGrupo: resultGrupo,
00323:           matchedMilhar: resultMilhar,
00324:           matchedAnimal: safeStr(
00325:             officialPrize?.animal || ""
00326:           ),
00327:         };
00328:       }
00329:     }
00330:   }
00331: 
00332:   return {
00333:     hitType: "miss",
00334:     hitScore: 0,
00335:     hitPosition: -1,
00336:     predictionPosition: -1,
00337:     resultPosition: -1,
00338:     podiumMedal: "",
00339:     matchedValue: "",

00457: export async function loadTop3PredictionDay({
00458:   lotteryKey,
00459:   targetYmd,
00460:   schedule,
00461: }) {
00462:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00463:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00464: 
00465:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return [];
00466: 
00467:   const hours = Array.from(
00468:     new Set(
00469:       (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
00470:         .map(normalizeHour)
00471:         .filter(Boolean)
00472:     )
00473:   );
00474: 
00475:   const snapshots = await Promise.all(
00476:     hours.map(async (hour) => {
00477:       const ref = predictionRef({
00478:         lotteryKey: lottery,
00479:         targetYmd: ymd,
00480:         targetHour: hour,
00481:       });
00482: 
00483:       if (!ref) return null;
00484: 
00485:       try {
00486:         const snap = await getDoc(ref);
00487:         if (!snap.exists()) return null;
00488: 
00489:         return {
00490:           id: snap.id,
00491:           ...snap.data(),
00492:         };
00493:       } catch {
00494:         return null;
00495:       }
00496:     })
00497:   );
00498: 
00499:   return snapshots
00500:     .filter(Boolean)
00501:     .sort((a, b) => {
00502:       return normalizeHour(a?.targetHour).localeCompare(
00503:         normalizeHour(b?.targetHour)
00504:       );
00505:     });
00506: }

00092: 
00093: function drawTs(draw) {
00094:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00095:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00096: 
00097:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00098: 
00099:   const [Y, M, D] = String(y).split("-").map(Number);
00100:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
00101: 
00102:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
00103:     return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00104:   }
00105: 
00106:   return Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
00107: }
00108: 
00109: function drawKey(draw) {
00110:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00111:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00112:   return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "";
00113: }
00114: 
00115: function hasDrawAtHour(draws, hourBucket) {
00116:   const target = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
00117:   if (!target) return false;
00118: 
00119:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some((d) => {
00120:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00121:     return h === target;
00122:   });
00123: }
00124: 
00125: function mergeBaseIntoRange(rangeDraws, baseDraw) {
00126:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
00127:   const key = drawKey(baseDraw);
00128: 
00129:   if (!key) return list;
00130: 
00131:   const map = new Map();
00132: 
00133:   for (const d of list) {
00134:     const k = drawKey(d);
00135:     if (k) map.set(k, d);
00136:   }
00137: 
00138:   if (!map.has(key)) {
00139:     map.set(key, baseDraw);
00140:   }
00141: 
00142:   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
00143: }
00144:

00148: 
00149:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
00150: 
00151:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
00152:     lotteryKey,
00153:     ymd: y,
00154:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,

01544:         if (retryCount > TOP3_RECONCILE_MAX_RETRIES) {
01545:           top3ReconcileRunKeys.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01546:           top3ReconcileRetryCounts.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01547: 
01548:           if (debugTop3) {
01549:             console.warn(
01550:               "[TOP3 FIRESTORE RECONCILE RETRIES EXHAUSTED]",
01551:               {
01552:                 reconcileRunKey,
01553:                 retryCount: retryCount - 1,
01554:                 historyLength: reconciledHistory.length,
01555:               }
01556:             );
01557:           }
01558: 
01559:           return;
01560:         }
01561: 
01562:         top3ReconcileRetryCounts.set(
01563:           reconcileRunKey,
01564:           retryCount
01565:         );
01566: 
01567:         window.setTimeout(() => {
01568:           top3ReconcileRunKeys.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01569: 
01570:           if (alive) {
01571:             setReconcileRetryNonce((value) => value + 1);
01572:           }
01573:         }, TOP3_RECONCILE_RETRY_DELAY_MS);
01574:       })
01575:       .catch((error) => {
01576:         top3ReconcileRunKeys.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01577:         top3ReconcileRetryCounts.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01578: 
01579:         if (debugTop3) {
01580:           console.warn("[TOP3 FIRESTORE RECONCILE]", error);
01581:         }
01582:       });
01583: 
01584:     return () => {
01585:       alive = false;
01586:     };
01587:   }, [
01588:     todayDraws,
01589:     rangeDraws,

01591:     timelineYmd,
01592:     scheduleKey,
01593:     debugTop3,

00144: function getTop3Log() {
00145:   try {
00146:     const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("top3_log") || "[]");
00147:     return normalizeAndDedupeLog(raw);
00148:   } catch {
00149:     return [];

00154:   try {
00155:     const normalized = normalizeAndDedupeLog(log).slice(-200);
00156:     localStorage.setItem("top3_log", JSON.stringify(normalized));

00160: function registerPrediction({
00161:   targetKey,
00162:   targetYmd,
00163:   targetHour,
00164:   picks,
00165:   snapshot = [],
00166:   engineVersion = "",
00167: }) {
00168:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00169:   const hour = normHour(targetHour);
00170:   const normalizedKey = targetKey || makeKey(ymd, hour);
00171: 
00172:   if (!isYMD(ymd) || !hour || !normalizedKey) return;
00173: 
00174:   const normalizedPicks = normalizePicks(picks);
00175:   if (!normalizedPicks.length) return;
00176: 
00177:   const log = getTop3Log();
00178: 
00179:   const idx = log.findIndex(
00180:     (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(normalizedKey)
00181:   );
00182: 
00183:   const status = resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, normalizedPicks);
00184: 
00185:   if (idx >= 0) {
00186:     const prev = log[idx] || {};
00187: 
00188:     if (prev.result != null || prev.status === "validated") return;
00189: 
00190:     log[idx] = {
00191:       ...prev,
00192:       targetKey: normalizedKey,
00193:       target: { ymd, hour },
00194:       picks: normalizedPicks,
00195:       result: null,
00196:       hit: null,
00197:       snapshot:
00198:         Array.isArray(prev?.snapshot) && prev.snapshot.length
00199:           ? prev.snapshot
00200:           : Array.isArray(snapshot)
00201:             ? snapshot
00202:             : [],
00203:       engineVersion:
00204:         safeStr(prev?.engineVersion || engineVersion || "V3_STATISTICAL"),
00205:       createdAt: prev.createdAt || Date.now(),
00206:       status,
00207:     };
00208:   } else {
00209:     log.push({
00210:       targetKey: normalizedKey,
00211:       target: { ymd, hour },
00212:       picks: normalizedPicks,
00213:       result: null,
00214:       hit: null,
00215:       snapshot: Array.isArray(snapshot) ? snapshot : [],
00216:       engineVersion: safeStr(engineVersion || "V3_STATISTICAL"),
00217:       createdAt: Date.now(),
00218:       status,
00219:     });
00220:   }
00221: 
00222:   saveTop3Log(log);
00223: }
00224:

00228:   const result = normalizeResult(resultGrupo);
00229: 
00230:   if (!normalizedTargetKey || result == null) return;
00231: 
00232:   const idx = log.findIndex(
00233:     (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(normalizedTargetKey)
00234:   );
00235: 
00236:   if (idx === -1) return;
00237: 
00238:   const picks = normalizePicks(log[idx]?.picks);
00239: 
00240:   log[idx] = {
00241:     ...log[idx],
00242:     picks,
00243:     result,
00244:     hit: picks.includes(result),
00245:     validatedAt: Date.now(),
00246:     status: "validated",
00247:   };
00248: 
00249:   saveTop3Log(log);
00250: }

00259:     pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd && normHour(pickDrawHour(d)) === hour;
00260:

00262:     (Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : []).find(sameTarget) || null;
00263: 
00264:   const realRange =
00265:     (Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : []).find(sameTarget) || null;

00277:     if (!entry || entry.result != null) return entry;

00280:     const targetHour = normHour(entry?.target?.hour || "");

00290:     });
00291: 
00292:     if (!real) return entry;
00293:

00295:     if (result == null) return entry;

00298: 
00299:     changed = true;
00300:

00302:       ...entry,
00303:       picks,
00304:       result,

00306:       validatedAt: Date.now(),

00006: function extractPrizeMilharByPosition(slot, position) {
00007:   const prizes = Array.isArray(slot?.prizes)
00008:     ? slot.prizes
00009:     : [];
00010: 
00011:   const prize =
00012:     prizes.find(
00013:       (item) => Number(item?.position) === Number(position)
00014:     ) ||
00015:     prizes[Number(position) - 1] ||
00016:     null;
00017: 
00018:   if (!prize) return "";
00019: 
00020:   const digits = String(
00021:     prize?.numero ??
00022:       prize?.milhar ??
00023:       prize?.number ??
00024:       prize?.valor ??
00025:       ""
00026:   ).replace(/\D/g, "");
00027: 
00028:   return digits
00029:     ? digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0")
00030:     : "";
00031: }

00037: function getPrizeGrupoByPosition(slot, position) {
00038:   const persisted = Array.isArray(
00039:     slot?.resultTop3Groups
00040:   )
00041:     ? Number(slot.resultTop3Groups[position - 1])
00042:     : NaN;
00043: 
00044:   if (
00045:     Number.isFinite(persisted) &&
00046:     persisted >= 1 &&
00047:     persisted <= 25
00048:   ) {
00049:     return persisted;
00050:   }
00051: 
00052:   const prizes = Array.isArray(slot?.prizes)
00053:     ? slot.prizes
00054:     : [];
00055: 
00056:   const prize =
00057:     prizes.find(
00058:       (item) => Number(item?.position) === Number(position)
00059:     ) ||
00060:     prizes[Number(position) - 1] ||
00061:     null;
00062: 
00063:   const direct = Number(
00064:     prize?.grupo ??
00065:       prize?.group ??
00066:       prize?.animal_grupo ??
00067:       prize?.grupo2
00068:   );
00069: 
00070:   if (
00071:     Number.isFinite(direct) &&
00072:     direct >= 1 &&
00073:     direct <= 25
00074:   ) {
00075:     return direct;
00076:   }
